Source: `dump/live-all.json` (29 use_figma pages, 1098 live boards, 10789 reaction lines). "Live" = visible top-level frame not prefixed ARCHIVE/REFERENCE/PROPOSED/LIBRARY/AUDIT/CHANGELOG/QA/FRAGMENT/START HERE/VERIFY, in a non-archive section (REFERENCE sections contribute only their CURRENT DESIGN/STATE boards), ≥240×180 except in the two OVERLAYS sections. Parked boards are counted in `dump/README.txt`; names not retained (budget).

Page state at dump time: 59 sections · 1,812 top-level frames (up from 1,394 on 09-15). Two `OVERLAYS · wrapped 19 Sep 2026` sections (388 frames) and a **new full-screen Brand workspace (nodes 7315–7318, boards stamped "superseded 21 Sep 2026")** were added by another session after the 09-16 audits — the rail Brand item now points at `7315:80955`.

Class: CURRENT = `CURRENT DESIGN ·` live design · STATE = `STATE ·` designed state · LAUNCHER = `STATES ·` per-section launcher · MENU/DETAIL = popover/menu boards · OTHER = unprefixed live boards (Assets task states, Surface-contract docs, etc.). Family = first name segment (raw; the gap matrix consolidates these into the ~16 v3 surface families).
